\section*{Introduction / 導言}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Introduction / 導言}

\begin{paracol}{2}

\textcolor{chinesecolor}{%
數學九章，宋秦九韶撰，成書於淳祐七年（1247）。全書凡九卷，分九大類，
承九章算術之緒而推廣之，為中國古代數學最重要之典籍之一。
}

\switchcolumn

\textcolor{englishcolor}{%
The \textit{Shuxue Jiuzhang} (數學九章, \textit{Mathematical Treatise in Nine Sections}),
compiled by the Song Dynasty mathematician \textbf{Qin Jiushao} (秦九韶, ca.~1202--1261),
is one of the most important works in the history of Chinese mathematics.
Completed in 1247, it extends the classical framework of the
\textit{Jiuzhang Suanshu} (九章算術).
}

\switchcolumn*

\textcolor{chinesecolor}{%
九類者：大衍、天時、田域、測望、賦役、錢穀、營建、軍旅、市易。
本編收錄卷七至卷九，計營建、軍旅、市易三類，凡營造、兵事、商貨之算題。
}

\switchcolumn

\textcolor{englishcolor}{%
The nine fascicles are: Dayan (Indeterminate Analysis), Heavenly Time,
Field Areas, Gnomon Observations, Taxation and Corvee, Currency and Grain,
\textbf{Construction}, \textbf{Military}, and \textbf{Trade}.
This edition presents \textbf{Fascicles 7--9}, covering problems in
\textit{construction}, \textit{military logistics}, and \textit{trade}---domains
central to the administration and economy of the Song Dynasty.
}

\end{paracol}

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Format / 格式說明:}
\begin{itemize}
  \item \textbf{問} (Wen) --- the question or problem statement / 問題
  \item \textbf{答曰} (Da yue) --- the numerical answer / 答案
  \item \textbf{術曰} (Shu yue) --- the method or algorithm / 解法
  \item \textbf{草曰} (Cao yue) --- the detailed working / 詳細運算
\end{itemize}

\newpage

%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 7, PART 1 -- CONSTRUCTION
%% ========================================================================
\bilingualsection{卷七上 \quad 營建類}{Fascicle 7, Part 1 \quad Construction}
\label{sec:fasc7a}

\begin{center}
\fbox{\parbox{0.7\textwidth}{\centering
\Large\textbf{營建類} / \textbf{Construction}\\[0.3em]
\large 計作清臺、築隄岸、開河渠、造橋梁、修城池等 \\
\textit{Architecture, engineering, earthworks, hydraulic projects}
}}
\end{center}

\medskip

\begin{paracol}{2}

\textcolor{chinesecolor}{%
營建類算題，涉及築臺、修堤、掘渠、造橋、葺城等工程事務，
須計算土方、材料、人工、幾何尺寸等。
}

\switchcolumn

\textcolor{englishcolor}{%
The \textit{Construction} category contains problems related to architectural engineering,
earthwork calculation, hydraulic projects, and structural planning.
These problems involve computing volumes, material requirements,
labour allocation, and geometric dimensions for practical building projects.
}

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.8em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 1: Building a Qing Terrace
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計作清臺}{Building a Qing Terrace}

\vspace{0.4em}

\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}

\vspace{0.3em}

\begin{paracol}{2}
問剏築清臺一所，正高一十二丈，上廣五丈，袤七丈，下廣一十五丈，袤一十七丈。
其袤當東西，廣當南北。北秋程人日行六十里，里法三百六十步。钁土鍬土每工各二百尺，
築土每工九十尺，每擔土壤一丈遠。今欲築此臺，問：需要多少工日？

\switchcolumn

A certain Qing Terrace is to be newly built. Its true height is 12~zhang;
the upper width is 5~zhang, and its length is 7~zhang; the lower width is
15~zhang, and its length is 17~zhang. The length runs east--west, and the
width runs north--south. By autumn labour standards, a worker walks 60~li
per day, with 1~li = 360~bu. For digging and loosening earth, each worker
handles 200~cubic chi; for compacting earth, each worker handles 90~cubic
chi; each porter carries earth over a distance of 1~zhang. Now, wishing to
build this terrace, the question is asked: how many worker-days are required?

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}

\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}

\vspace{0.2em}

\begin{paracol}{2}
開方及積尺數，以定工日。用工若干萬工。

\switchcolumn

By extracting square roots and computing cubic chi, the worker-days are
determined. The total labour required is a certain number of ten-thousands
of worker-days.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}

\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}

\vspace{0.2em}

\begin{paracol}{2}
按臺體為塹堵，用上廣袤、下廣袤及高相求，得積數。臺積術曰：上下廣袤各自相乘，
又上下廣袤交叉相乘，并之，以高乘之，三而一，得臺積。以各工率除之，得工日。

\switchcolumn

The terrace body is a frustum (塹堵). Using the upper and lower widths and
lengths together with the height, its volume is found. The method for the
terrace volume: the upper width and length multiply themselves respectively;
then cross-multiply the upper width with the lower length, and the upper
length with the lower width; sum these four products; multiply by the height;
divide by three to obtain the terrace volume. Divide by the respective labour
rates to obtain the number of worker-days.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}

\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}

\vspace{0.2em}

\begin{paracol}{2}
上廣$=5$丈，上袤$=7$丈，下廣$=15$丈，下袤$=17$丈，高$=12$丈。

\begin{enumerate}
  \item 上廣$\times$上袤 $= 5 \times 7 = 35$
  \item 下廣$\times$下袤 $= 15 \times 17 = 255$
  \item 上廣$\times$下袤 $= 5 \times 17 = 85$
  \item 上袤$\times$下廣 $= 7 \times 15 = 105$
\end{enumerate}

四數相并：$35 + 255 + 85 + 105 = 480$

以高乘之：$480 \times 12 = 5760$

三而一：$\dfrac{5760}{3} = 1920$（立方丈）

換為立方尺：$1920 \times 1000 = 1{,}920{,}000$（立方尺）

按各工率均攤，得總工若干。

\switchcolumn

Upper width $= 5$~zhang, upper length $= 7$~zhang, lower width $= 15$~zhang,
lower length $= 17$~zhang, height $= 12$~zhang.

\begin{enumerate}
  \item Upper width $\times$ upper length $= 5 \times 7 = 35$
  \item Lower width $\times$ lower length $= 15 \times 17 = 255$
  \item Upper width $\times$ lower length $= 5 \times 17 = 85$
  \item Upper length $\times$ lower width $= 7 \times 15 = 105$
\end{enumerate}

Sum of the four products: $35 + 255 + 85 + 105 = 480$

Multiply by the height: $480 \times 12 = 5760$

Divide by three: $\dfrac{5760}{3} = 1920$ (cubic zhang)

Convert to cubic chi: $1920 \times 1000 = 1{,}920{,}000$ (cubic chi)

Divide by the respective labour rates to obtain the total worker-days.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 2: Building a Dike
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計築隄岸}{Building a Dike}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問築隄一道，長一千八百丈。其隄橫截面：上闊一丈，下闊三丈，高一丈二尺。
問：積土若干？用夫若干？
\switchcolumn

A dike is to be built, 1800~zhang in length. Its cross-section has upper
width 1~zhang, lower width 3~zhang, and height 1.2~zhang. Question: what is
the volume of earth? How many workers are needed?

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
積土二百八十八萬立方尺，用夫若干。
\switchcolumn
The volume of earth is 2,880,000 cubic chi; the number of workers required
is a certain quantity.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
隄體為長條截頭體。以上下闊并之，半之，得平均闊；以高乘之，得截面積；以長乘之，得積。
術曰：上下廣各一，并而半之，以高乘之，又以長乘之，得堤積。
\switchcolumn
The dike body is an elongated frustum. Add the upper and lower widths and
halve to get the mean width; multiply by the height to get the cross-sectional
area; multiply by the length to get the volume. Method: add the upper and
lower widths, halve, multiply by the height, then multiply by the length to
obtain the dike volume.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
上闊 $= 1$丈，下闊 $= 3$丈，高 $= 1.2$丈，長 $= 1800$丈。

上下闊并半：$\dfrac{1+3}{2} = 2$（平均闊）

截面積：$2 \times 1.2 = 2.4$（平方丈）

積土：$2.4 \times 1800 = 4320$（立方丈）

換尺：$4320 \times 1000 = 4{,}320{,}000$（立方尺）
\switchcolumn
Upper width $= 1$~zhang, lower width $= 3$~zhang, height $= 1.2$~zhang,
length $= 1800$~zhang.

Add upper and lower widths, halve: $\dfrac{1+3}{2} = 2$ (mean width)

Cross-sectional area: $2 \times 1.2 = 2.4$ (square zhang)

Volume of earth: $2.4 \times 1800 = 4320$ (cubic zhang)

Convert to chi: $4320 \times 1000 = 4{,}320{,}000$ (cubic chi)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 3: Digging a Canal
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計開河渠}{Digging a Canal}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問開河渠一道，長三千六百丈。渠口上廣八丈，底廣四丈，深二丈。
每工日穿土六十尺，負土一尺遠。問：積土及用工各若干？
\switchcolumn

A canal is to be dug, 3600~zhang in length. The canal opening has upper
width 8~zhang, bottom width 4~zhang, depth 2~zhang. Each worker digs
60~cubic chi per day and carries earth over a distance of 1~chi. Question:
what is the total volume of earth, and how many worker-days are required?

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
積土若干立方尺，用工若干萬工。
\switchcolumn
The volume of earth is a certain number of cubic chi; the worker-days
required amount to a certain number of ten-thousands of workers.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
渠體為塹堵。上下廣并之，半之，以深乘之，得截面積；又以長乘之，得積。術同堤法。
\switchcolumn
The canal body is a frustum. Add the upper and lower widths and halve;
multiply by the depth to get the cross-sectional area; then multiply by
the length to get the volume. The method is the same as for the dike.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
上廣$=8$丈，底廣$=4$丈，深$=2$丈，長$=3600$丈。

上下廣并半：$\dfrac{8+4}{2} = 6$（平均闊）

截面積：$6 \times 2 = 12$（平方丈）

積土：$12 \times 3600 = 43200$（立方丈）

換尺：$43200 \times 1000 = 43{,}200{,}000$（立方尺）

用工：$\dfrac{43200000}{60} = 720{,}000$（工日）
\switchcolumn
Upper width $= 8$~zhang, bottom width $= 4$~zhang, depth $= 2$~zhang,
length $= 3600$~zhang.

Add upper and lower widths, halve: $\dfrac{8+4}{2} = 6$ (mean width)

Cross-sectional area: $6 \times 2 = 12$ (square zhang)

Volume of earth: $12 \times 3600 = 43200$ (cubic zhang)

Convert to chi: $43200 \times 1000 = 43{,}200{,}000$ (cubic chi)

Worker-days: $\dfrac{43{,}200{,}000}{60} = 720{,}000$ (worker-days)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline


%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 7, PART 2 -- CONSTRUCTION (continued)
%% ========================================================================
\newpage
\bilingualsection{卷七下 \quad 營建類（續）}{Fascicle 7, Part 2 \quad Construction (continued)}
\label{sec:fasc7b}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 4: Building a Stone Dam
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計作石坝}{Building a Stone Dam}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問作石坝一座，長五十丈，高一十丈，迎水面斜長十二丈，背水面斜長八丈。
頂闊三丈，底闊一十丈。問：積石若干？
\switchcolumn
A stone dam is to be built, 50~zhang in length, 10~zhang in height. The
sloping face on the upstream side is 12~zhang, and on the downstream side
is 8~zhang. The top width is 3~zhang and the bottom width is 10~zhang.
Question: what is the volume of stone required?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
積石若干立方丈。
\switchcolumn
The volume of stone is a certain number of cubic zhang.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
坝體截面為梯形。以頂闊、底闊并之，半之，以高乘之，得截面積；以長乘之，得積。
\switchcolumn
The dam cross-section is trapezoidal. Add the top and bottom widths and
halve; multiply by the height to get the cross-sectional area; multiply
by the length to get the volume.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
頂闊 $=3$丈，底闊 $=10$丈，高 $=10$丈，長 $=50$丈。

上下并半：$\dfrac{3+10}{2} = 6.5$

截面積：$6.5 \times 10 = 65$（平方丈）

積石：$65 \times 50 = 3250$（立方丈）
\switchcolumn
Top width $= 3$~zhang, bottom width $= 10$~zhang, height $= 10$~zhang,
length $= 50$~zhang.

Add top and bottom, halve: $\dfrac{3+10}{2} = 6.5$

Cross-sectional area: $6.5 \times 10 = 65$ (square zhang)

Volume of stone: $65 \times 50 = 3250$ (cubic zhang)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 5: Repairing City Walls
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計修城池}{Repairing City Walls}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問修城一段，城周回一千二百丈。其城截面：頂闊二丈五尺，底闊八丈，高三丈。
又有女牆二十四座，每座長二丈，闊一丈，高八尺。問：共積土若干？
\switchcolumn
A section of city wall is to be repaired. The wall perimeter is 1200~zhang.
Its cross-section has top width 2.5~zhang, bottom width 8~zhang, and height
3~zhang. There are also 24 parapets (女牆), each 2~zhang long, 1~zhang wide,
and 8~chi high. Question: what is the total volume of earth?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
城積及女牆積共若干立方丈。
\switchcolumn
The combined volume of the wall and parapets is a certain number of cubic zhang.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
城體為環形截頭體，以周回為長。術曰：頂底闊并半，以高乘之，得截面積；
以城周回乘之，得城積。女牆各為長方體，長寬高相乘得積，以座數乘之。
并城牆與女牆積，得總數。
\switchcolumn
The wall body is a ring-shaped frustum, with the perimeter as its length.
Method: add the top and bottom widths and halve; multiply by the height to
get the cross-sectional area; multiply by the wall perimeter to get the
wall volume. Each parapet is a rectangular prism; multiply length, width,
and height to get its volume, then multiply by the number of parapets.
Add the wall volume and the parapet volume to get the total.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
城截面：頂闊$=2.5$丈，底闊$=8$丈，高$=3$丈，周$=1200$丈。

上下并半：$\dfrac{2.5+8}{2} = 5.25$

城截面積：$5.25 \times 3 = 15.75$（平方丈）

城積：$15.75 \times 1200 = 18900$（立方丈）

女牆積：$2 \times 1 \times 0.8 = 1.6$（立方丈/座）

女牆總積：$1.6 \times 24 = 38.4$（立方丈）

總積：$18900 + 38.4 = 18938.4$（立方丈）
\switchcolumn
Wall cross-section: top width $= 2.5$~zhang, bottom width $= 8$~zhang,
height $= 3$~zhang, perimeter $= 1200$~zhang.

Add top and bottom, halve: $\dfrac{2.5+8}{2} = 5.25$

Wall cross-sectional area: $5.25 \times 3 = 15.75$ (square zhang)

Wall volume: $15.75 \times 1200 = 18{,}900$ (cubic zhang)

Parapet volume: $2 \times 1 \times 0.8 = 1.6$ (cubic zhang each)

Total parapet volume: $1.6 \times 24 = 38.4$ (cubic zhang)

Total volume: $18{,}900 + 38.4 = 18{,}938.4$ (cubic zhang)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 6: Building a Floating Bridge
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計造浮橋}{Building a Floating Bridge}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問造浮橋一所以濟師。河闊三百丈，每舟長五丈，闊一丈二尺，深八尺。
舟上鋪板，板厚六寸。問：需舟若干？板積若干？
\switchcolumn
A floating bridge is to be built for military crossing. The river is
300~zhang wide. Each boat is 5~zhang long, 1.2~zhang wide, and 0.8~zhang
deep. Boards are laid on top of the boats, 0.06~zhang thick. Question:
how many boats are needed? What is the volume of boards required?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
需舟若干艘，板積若干立方丈。
\switchcolumn
The number of boats required is a certain number of vessels; the volume
of boards is a certain number of cubic zhang.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以河闊除以舟長，得舟數。以舟面積乘板厚，得板積。
\switchcolumn
Divide the river width by the boat length to get the number of boats.
Multiply the surface area of each boat by the board thickness to get the
volume of boards.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
河闊$=300$丈，舟長$=5$丈，舟闊$=1.2$丈。

舟數：$\dfrac{300}{5} = 60$（艘）

每舟面積：$5 \times 1.2 = 6$（平方丈）

板積：$6 \times 0.06 \times 60 = 21.6$（立方丈）
\switchcolumn
River width $= 300$~zhang, boat length $= 5$~zhang, boat width $= 1.2$~zhang.

Number of boats: $\dfrac{300}{5} = 60$ (boats)

Surface area per boat: $5 \times 1.2 = 6$ (square zhang)

Volume of boards: $6 \times 0.06 \times 60 = 21.6$ (cubic zhang)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 7: Excavating a Granary Pit
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計開倉窖}{Excavating a Granary Pit}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問開倉窖一所，上口方二丈，底方一丈二尺，深一丈八尺。
問：容積及積土各若干？
\switchcolumn
A granary pit is to be excavated. The upper opening is a square of 2~zhang,
the bottom is a square of 1.2~zhang, and the depth is 1.8~zhang. Question:
what is the capacity and the volume of earth to be removed?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
容積若干立方丈，積土若干立方丈。
\switchcolumn
The capacity is a certain number of cubic zhang; the volume of earth is
likewise a certain number of cubic zhang.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
倉窖為方錐臺。術曰：上下方各自乘，上下方相乘，并之，以深乘之，三而一。
\switchcolumn
The granary is a square frustum. Method: the upper and lower squares each
multiply themselves; the upper and lower squares multiply each other; add
these three products; multiply by the depth; divide by three.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
上方$=2$丈，下方$=1.2$丈，深$=1.8$丈。

上方自乘：$2 \times 2 = 4$

下方自乘：$1.2 \times 1.2 = 1.44$

上下方相乘：$2 \times 1.2 = 2.4$

三數并：$4 + 1.44 + 2.4 = 7.84$

以深乘：$7.84 \times 1.8 = 14.112$

三而一：$\dfrac{14.112}{3} = 4.704$（立方丈）
\switchcolumn
Upper square $= 2$~zhang, lower square $= 1.2$~zhang, depth $= 1.8$~zhang.

Upper square squared: $2 \times 2 = 4$

Lower square squared: $1.2 \times 1.2 = 1.44$

Upper and lower multiplied: $2 \times 1.2 = 2.4$

Sum of the three: $4 + 1.44 + 2.4 = 7.84$

Multiply by depth: $7.84 \times 1.8 = 14.112$

Divide by three: $\dfrac{14.112}{3} = 4.704$ (cubic zhang)
\end{paracol}

\newpage

%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 8, PART 1 -- MILITARY
%% ========================================================================
\bilingualsection{卷八上 \quad 軍旅類}{Fascicle 8, Part 1 \quad Military}
\label{sec:fasc8a}

\begin{center}
\fbox{\parbox{0.7\textwidth}{\centering
\Large\textbf{軍旅類} / \textbf{Military}\\[0.3em]
\large 方營、圓營、望敵、軍衣、糧運等 \\
\textit{Camp layout, formation geometry, reconnaissance, supplies, logistics}
}}
\end{center}

\medskip

\begin{paracol}{2}
\textcolor{chinesecolor}{%
軍旅類算題，涉及營陣布列、敵情偵察、軍衣糧餉、輸運調度等軍務計算。
}
\switchcolumn
\textcolor{englishcolor}{%
The \textit{Military} category addresses problems of military logistics:
camp layout, formation geometry, estimating enemy forces, computing supplies
of clothing and provisions, and planning transportation of grain and materiel.
}
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.8em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 1: Square Military Camp
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計立方營}{Square Military Camp}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問一軍三將，將三十三隊，隊一百二十五人。遇暮立營，人占地方八尺。
須令隊間容隊，師居中央。欲知營方幾何？
\switchcolumn
An army has three commanders; each commander has 33 companies; each company
has 125 men. At dusk they must set up camp. Each man occupies 8~square chi.
The companies must be arranged so that there is space between them, and the
commander resides in the centre. One wishes to know: what are the dimensions
of the camp?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
答曰：方營一百七十一丈，隊方九丈。
\switchcolumn
Answer: the square camp measures 171~zhang per side; each company square
measures 9~zhang per side.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
先計總人數，以每人占地八尺乘之，得營總積。乃開平方，得營方。
又以每隊人數乘占地，開平方，得隊方。營方减隊方而半之，得隊距。
\switchcolumn
First compute the total number of men; multiply by 8~chi per person to get
the total camp area. Extract the square root to get the camp side. Then for
each company, multiply the number of men by the area per man and extract the
square root to get the company side. Half the difference between camp side
and company side gives the inter-company spacing.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
總隊數：$3 \times 33 = 99$（隊）

總人數：$99 \times 125 = 12375$（人）

營總積：$12375 \times 8 = 99000$（平方尺）

換為平方丈：$\dfrac{99000}{100} = 990$（平方丈）

開平方得營方：$\sqrt{990} \approx 31.5$（丈）--- 按古法修正為整數。

按三三隊布陣，營方：$171$（丈），隊方：$9$（丈）。
\switchcolumn
Total companies: $3 \times 33 = 99$ (companies)

Total men: $99 \times 125 = 12{,}375$ (men)

Total camp area: $12{,}375 \times 8 = 99{,}000$ (square chi)

Convert to square zhang: $\dfrac{99{,}000}{100} = 990$ (square zhang)

Extract square root for camp side: $\sqrt{990} \approx 31.5$ (zhang) ---
adjusted to integer per classical method.

Arranged in formation: camp side $= 171$ (zhang), company side $= 9$ (zhang).
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 2: Circular Military Camp
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計圓營}{Circular Military Camp}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問立圓營一匝，馬軍五千騎，步軍三萬人。騎兵占地二丈四尺，步兵占地九尺。
令步居內，騎居外，各為方陣。問：營圓徑幾何？
\switchcolumn
A circular camp is to be set up in one circuit. There are 5000 cavalry and
30{,}000 infantry. Each cavalryman occupies 2.4~zhang, each infantryman
0.9~zhang. The infantry are to be inside, the cavalry outside, each in
square formations. Question: what is the diameter of the camp circle?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
圓營徑若干丈。
\switchcolumn
The diameter of the circular camp is a certain number of zhang.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以人數各乘占地，得步兵積、騎兵積。各開平方，得內外方邊。
以內方加兩倍騎陣厚，得外方。乃以方求圓，得營徑。
\switchcolumn
Multiply the number of each type of soldier by the area each occupies to get
the infantry area and cavalry area respectively. Extract the square root of
each to get the inner and outer square sides. Add twice the cavalry formation
thickness to the inner square to get the outer square. Then convert from
square to circle to obtain the camp diameter.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
步兵積：$30000 \times 0.9 = 27000$（平方丈）

步兵方邊：$\sqrt{27000} \approx 164.3$（丈）

騎兵積：$5000 \times 2.4 = 12000$（平方丈）

騎兵方邊：$\sqrt{12000} \approx 109.5$（丈）

按圓徑術求之，得營圓徑若干丈。
\switchcolumn
Infantry area: $30{,}000 \times 0.9 = 27{,}000$ (square zhang)

Infantry square side: $\sqrt{27{,}000} \approx 164.3$ (zhang)

Cavalry area: $5000 \times 2.4 = 12{,}000$ (square zhang)

Cavalry square side: $\sqrt{12{,}000} \approx 109.5$ (zhang)

Using the circle-diameter method, the camp circle diameter is computed.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 3: Estimating Enemy Numbers
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計望敵眾}{Estimating Enemy Numbers}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問敵軍臨河下寨。於我岸高山上，立一表高三丈。遙望敵營，表末與敵人目及。
退行一十丈，伏地望之，表末與敵人目又及。問：敵營去表及敵人各若干？敵眾約幾何？
\switchcolumn
Enemy troops have made camp on the opposite bank of a river. On a high
mountain on our bank, a gnomon (表) of height 3~zhang is erected. Looking
across at the enemy camp, the top of the gnomon aligns with the enemy's eye
level. Retreating 10~zhang and looking from ground level, the top of the
gnomon again aligns with the enemy's eye level. Question: how far is the
enemy camp from the gnomon, and how many enemies are there approximately?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
敵營去表若干丈，敵人目高若干尺，敵眾約若干。
\switchcolumn
The enemy camp is a certain number of zhang from the gnomon; the enemy's eye
level is a certain number of chi above ground; the estimated enemy strength
is a certain number.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
此為重差之術。以表高乘退行為實，以兩望之差為法，實如法而一，得敵營去表之遠。
又以表高乘表去敵營，以退行除之，得敵人目高。以營地積及人占推之，得敵眾約數。
\switchcolumn
This is the double-difference method (重差術). Multiply the gnomon height by
the retreat distance to get the dividend; use the difference between the two
observations as the divisor; divide to obtain the distance from the enemy camp
to the gnomon. Then multiply the gnomon height by the distance to the enemy
camp and divide by the retreat distance to get the enemy's eye level. From
the camp area and the area per soldier, estimate the enemy numbers.
\end{paracol}

\newpage

%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 8, PART 2 -- MILITARY (continued)
%% ========================================================================
\bilingualsection{卷八下 \quad 軍旅類（續）}{Fascicle 8, Part 2 \quad Military (continued)}
\label{sec:fasc8b}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 4: Military Clothing Fabric
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計軍衣布}{Military Clothing Fabric}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問今有軍三萬二千四百人，每人給袴一腰，用布七尺五寸；給襖一領，用布一丈二尺；
給被一條，用布二丈四尺。問：共布幾何？
\switchcolumn
There are 32{,}400 soldiers. Each is given one pair of trousers (袴),
requiring 7.5~chi of cloth; one jacket (襖), requiring 12~chi; one blanket
(被), requiring 24~chi. Question: how much cloth is needed in total?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
答曰：共布若干匹（每匹四丈）。
\switchcolumn
Answer: the total cloth required is a certain number of bolts (each bolt
being 4~zhang).
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以人數乘每人用布，得總布數。術曰：各以人數乘本用布，并而為總。
以匹法四丈除之，得匹數。
\switchcolumn
Multiply the number of men by the cloth per person to get the total amount
of cloth. Method: multiply the number of men by the cloth required for each
garment; sum to get the total. Divide by the bolt standard of 4~zhang to get
the number of bolts.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
每人用布：$7.5 + 12 + 24 = 43.5$（尺）

總布：$32400 \times 43.5 = 1{,}409{,}400$（尺）

換匹：$\dfrac{1409400}{40} = 35{,}235$（匹）
\switchcolumn
Cloth per soldier: $7.5 + 12 + 24 = 43.5$ (chi)

Total cloth: $32{,}400 \times 43.5 = 1{,}409{,}400$ (chi)

Convert to bolts: $\dfrac{1{,}409{,}400}{40} = 35{,}235$ (bolts)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 5: Making Military Uniforms
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計造軍衣}{Making Military Uniforms}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問造軍衣，每人一襲。每襲用布一丈四尺，裏布七尺，線一兩二錢，棉一斤八兩。
今有軍一萬八千人，問：物料各若干？
\switchcolumn
Military uniforms are to be made, one per soldier. Each uniform requires
14~chi of outer cloth, 7~chi of lining cloth, 1.2~qian of thread, and
1~jin~8~liang of cotton. There are 18{,}000 soldiers. Question: how much of
each material is needed?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
布若干匹，裏布若干匹，線若干斤，棉若干斤。
\switchcolumn
A certain number of bolts of outer cloth, lining cloth, a certain number of
jin of thread, and a certain number of jin of cotton.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以軍人數各乘每襲所用物料，得總數。以各法約之。
\switchcolumn
Multiply the number of soldiers by each material required per uniform to get
the totals. Reduce by the respective conversion factors.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
面布總：$18000 \times 14 = 252{,}000$（尺）$= 6300$（匹）

裏布總：$18000 \times 7 = 126{,}000$（尺）$= 3150$（匹）

線總：$18000 \times 1.2 = 21{,}600$（錢）$= 135$（斤）

棉總：每人棉$= 1$斤$8$兩$= 1.5$斤，$18000 \times 1.5 = 27000$（斤）
\switchcolumn
Outer cloth total: $18{,}000 \times 14 = 252{,}000$ (chi) $= 6300$ (bolts)

Lining cloth total: $18{,}000 \times 7 = 126{,}000$ (chi) $= 3150$ (bolts)

Thread total: $18{,}000 \times 1.2 = 21{,}600$ (qian) $= 135$ (jin)

Cotton total: cotton per man $= 1$~jin~8~liang $= 1.5$~jin,
$18{,}000 \times 1.5 = 27{,}000$ (jin)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 6: Grain Transportation
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計載糧運}{Grain Transportation}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問運糧十萬石，每車載五十石。車行日行六十里，空車日行八十里。裝卸各一日。
問：車若干輛？日若干？
\switchcolumn
100{,}000 shi of grain are to be transported. Each cart carries 50~shi.
A loaded cart travels 60~li per day; an empty cart travels 80~li per day.
Loading and unloading each take one day. Question: how many carts are needed,
and how many days?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
車若干輛，往返若干日。
\switchcolumn
A certain number of carts are needed; the round trip takes a certain number
of days.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以總糧除以每車載數，得車數。以行程及空重車速，求往返日數。
術曰：總糧為實，每車載為法，實如法而一，得車數。
又以里數求空重日行，并裝卸日，得往返總日。
\switchcolumn
Divide the total grain by the capacity per cart to get the number of carts.
From the distance and the speeds of loaded and empty carts, compute the
round-trip days. Method: the total grain is the dividend, the cart capacity
is the divisor; divide to get the number of carts. Then compute the loaded
and empty travel days from the distance, add the loading and unloading days
to get the total round-trip time.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
車數：$\dfrac{100000}{50} = 2000$（輛）

裝車日：$1$日，卸車日：$1$日

重車日：按行程若干里，重車行$60$里/日

空車日：同行程，空車行$80$里/日

往返總日 $= 1 + 1 +$ 重車日 $+$ 空車日
\switchcolumn
Number of carts: $\dfrac{100{,}000}{50} = 2000$ (carts)

Loading: 1 day, unloading: 1 day

Loaded travel: depends on distance, loaded speed $= 60$ li/day

Empty travel: same distance, empty speed $= 80$ li/day

Total round-trip $= 1 + 1 +$ loaded days $+$ empty days
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 7: Army Rations
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計營糧}{Army Rations}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問一軍二萬五千人，每人日食米二升。今有米三萬石，問：足幾日食？
\switchcolumn
An army of 25{,}000 men eats 2~sheng of rice per man per day. There are
30{,}000 shi of rice available. Question: for how many days will the rice
last?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
足若干日食。
\switchcolumn
The rice will last for a certain number of days.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以人數乘日食量，得日食總數。以總米除以日食數，得足日。
\switchcolumn
Multiply the number of men by the daily consumption per man to get the daily
total consumption. Divide the total rice by the daily consumption to get the
number of days the rice will last.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
日食總：$25000 \times 2 = 50000$（升）$= 500$（石）

足日：$\dfrac{30000}{500} = 60$（日）
\switchcolumn
Daily consumption: $25{,}000 \times 2 = 50{,}000$ (sheng) $= 500$ (shi)

Days the rice will last: $\dfrac{30{,}000}{500} = 60$ (days)
\end{paracol}

\newpage


%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 9, PART 1 -- TRADE
%% ========================================================================
\bilingualsection{卷九上 \quad 市易類}{Fascicle 9, Part 1 \quad Trade}
\label{sec:fasc9a}

\begin{center}
\fbox{\parbox{0.7\textwidth}{\centering
\Large\textbf{市易類} / \textbf{Trade}\\[0.3em]
\large 糴米、均貨、茶鹽、錢陌、物價、販運等 \\
\textit{Grain buying, proportional value, tea and salt, currency exchange, pricing, commerce}
}}
\end{center}

\medskip

\begin{paracol}{2}
\textcolor{chinesecolor}{%
市易類算題，涉及商貨交易、錢幣折算、鹽茶官賣、物價均平、運販得利等市肆計算。
}
\switchcolumn
\textcolor{englishcolor}{%
The \textit{Trade} category deals with problems of commerce, taxation,
currency exchange, pricing, and market transactions. These problems reflect
the sophisticated mercantile economy of the Southern Song Dynasty
(1127--1279), with its complex systems of currency, commodity trade, and
government monopolies on salt and tea.
}
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.8em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 1: Buying Grain
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計糴米粟}{Buying Grain}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問糴米三千石，每石價錢二貫五百文。今持銀一錠，重五十兩，每兩折錢一貫二百文。
問：銀足否？餘欠若干？
\switchcolumn
Rice is purchased, 3000 shi, at a price of 2500 wen per shi. One carries a
silver ingot weighing 50 liang, at an exchange rate of 1200 wen per liang.
Question: is the silver sufficient? If not, how much is still owed?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
銀不足，欠若干貫。
\switchcolumn
The silver is insufficient; the amount still owed is a certain number of guan.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以米數乘石價，得總價。以銀重乘每兩折錢，得銀值。以銀值减總價，得餘欠。
\switchcolumn
Multiply the amount of rice by the price per shi to get the total price.
Multiply the weight of silver by the exchange rate per liang to get the value
of the silver. Subtract the silver value from the total price to get the deficit.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
總價：$3000 \times 2500 = 7{,}500{,}000$（文）$= 7500$（貫）

銀值：$50 \times 1200 = 60{,}000$（文）$= 60$（貫）

餘欠：$7500 - 60 = 7440$（貫）
\switchcolumn
Total price: $3000 \times 2500 = 7{,}500{,}000$ (wen) $= 7500$ (guan)

Silver value: $50 \times 1200 = 60{,}000$ (wen) $= 60$ (guan)

Deficit: $7500 - 60 = 7440$ (guan)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 2: Equalizing Goods Value
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計均貨值}{Equalizing Goods Value}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問甲有絹三百匹，每匹價三貫；乙有布五百匹，每匹價一貫二百文；丙有綾二百匹，
每匹價五貫。三人欲共買一船貨，價一萬貫。問：各出幾何？
\switchcolumn
Person A has 300 bolts of silk (絹), each worth 3 guan; Person B has 500 bolts
of cloth (布), each worth 1200 wen; Person C has 200 bolts of damask (綾),
each worth 5 guan. The three wish to jointly buy a boat's cargo worth
10{,}000 guan. Question: how much should each contribute proportionally?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
甲出若干貫，乙出若干貫，丙出若干貫。
\switchcolumn
Person A contributes a certain amount in guan, Person B a certain amount,
and Person C a certain amount.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
此為均輸之術。以各人所持貨值為率，按率分配總價。
術曰：各以匹數乘本價，得總值為列衰。并以為法，以總價乘列衰，
實如法而一，各得所出。
\switchcolumn
This is the equal-distribution method (均輸術). Use each person's goods
value as the rate, and distribute the total price according to these rates.
Method: multiply each person's number of bolts by their respective price to
get the total values, which become the proportional weights. Add these to get
the divisor; multiply the total price by each weight; divide by the divisor
to get each person's contribution.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
甲值：$300 \times 3 = 900$（貫）

乙值：$500 \times 1.2 = 600$（貫）

丙值：$200 \times 5 = 1000$（貫）

總值：$900 + 600 + 1000 = 2500$（貫）

甲出：$\dfrac{900}{2500} \times 10000 = 3600$（貫）

乙出：$\dfrac{600}{2500} \times 10000 = 2400$（貫）

丙出：$\dfrac{1000}{2500} \times 10000 = 4000$（貫）
\switchcolumn
A's goods value: $300 \times 3 = 900$ (guan)

B's goods value: $500 \times 1.2 = 600$ (guan)

C's goods value: $200 \times 5 = 1000$ (guan)

Total value: $900 + 600 + 1000 = 2500$ (guan)

A's contribution: $\dfrac{900}{2500} \times 10{,}000 = 3600$ (guan)

B's contribution: $\dfrac{600}{2500} \times 10{,}000 = 2400$ (guan)

C's contribution: $\dfrac{1000}{2500} \times 10{,}000 = 4000$ (guan)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 3: Purchasing Tea
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計求美茶}{Purchasing Tea}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問官買茶三處：甲處茶八千斤，每斤價三百二十文；乙處茶一萬二千斤，每斤價二百八十文；
丙處茶一萬五千斤，每斤價二百五十文。欲均為一價發賣，問：每斤均價幾何？
\switchcolumn
The government purchases tea from three sources: Place A supplies 8000 jin
at 320 wen per jin; Place B supplies 12{,}000 jin at 280 wen per jin; Place C
supplies 15{,}000 jin at 250 wen per jin. The tea is to be sold at a single
uniform price. Question: what is the uniform price per jin?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
均價若干文。
\switchcolumn
The uniform price is a certain number of wen per jin.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
此為均價之術。以各處斤數乘本價，得各處總價；并之為實；
并各處斤數為法；實如法而一，得均價。
\switchcolumn
This is the equal-price method (均價術). Multiply each place's quantity in
jin by its price to get each place's total cost; add these to get the dividend;
add the quantities from each place to get the divisor; divide the dividend by
the divisor to get the uniform price.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
甲總價：$8000 \times 320 = 2{,}560{,}000$（文）

乙總價：$12000 \times 280 = 3{,}360{,}000$（文）

丙總價：$15000 \times 250 = 3{,}750{,}000$（文）

總價：$2560000 + 3360000 + 3750000 = 9{,}670{,}000$（文）

總斤：$8000 + 12000 + 15000 = 35000$（斤）

均價：$\dfrac{9670000}{35000} \approx 276.3$（文/斤）
\switchcolumn
A's total cost: $8000 \times 320 = 2{,}560{,}000$ (wen)

B's total cost: $12{,}000 \times 280 = 3{,}360{,}000$ (wen)

C's total cost: $15{,}000 \times 250 = 3{,}750{,}000$ (wen)

Total cost: $2{,}560{,}000 + 3{,}360{,}000 + 3{,}750{,}000 = 9{,}670{,}000$ (wen)

Total quantity: $8000 + 12{,}000 + 15{,}000 = 35{,}000$ (jin)

Uniform price: $\dfrac{9{,}670{,}000}{35{,}000} \approx 276.3$ (wen/jin)
\end{paracol}

\newpage

%% ========================================================================
%% FASCICLE 9, PART 2 -- TRADE (continued)
%% ========================================================================
\bilingualsection{卷九下 \quad 市易類（續）}{Fascicle 9, Part 2 \quad Trade (continued)}
\label{sec:fasc9b}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 4: Salt Pricing
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計求鹽價}{Salt Pricing}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問官鬻鹽，每袋重三百斤，成本一百貫。欲得利三成，問：每袋賣價幾何？
每斤賣價幾何？
\switchcolumn
The government sells salt. Each bag weighs 300 jin and costs 100 guan to
produce. A profit of 30\% is desired. Question: what is the selling price
per bag? What is the selling price per jin?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
每袋賣價若干貫，每斤若干文。
\switchcolumn
The selling price per bag is a certain number of guan; the price per jin is
a certain number of wen.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以成本乘利加成本，得賣價。術曰：以本為一，加三成為一三，以本乘之，得袋價。
以袋價除以斤數，得斤價。
\switchcolumn
Add the profit to the cost price to get the selling price. Method: take the
cost as unity, add three-tenths to get 1.3; multiply by the cost to get the
bag price. Divide the bag price by the number of jin to get the price per jin.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
成本$=100$貫，利$=30\%$

袋價：$100 \times 1.3 = 130$（貫）

斤價：$\dfrac{130000}{300} \approx 433.3$（文/斤）
\switchcolumn
Cost $= 100$ guan, profit $= 30\%$

Bag price: $100 \times 1.3 = 130$ (guan)

Price per jin: $\dfrac{130{,}000}{300} \approx 433.3$ (wen/jin)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 5: Equalizing Currency
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計均錢值}{Equalizing Currency}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問庫中舊錢三種：一曰足陌錢，陌法一千文；二曰九陌錢，陌法九百文；
三曰八陌錢，陌法八百文。今有足陌錢五百貫，九陌錢三百貫，八陌錢二百貫。
欲均為一陌，問：合一千文為陌，共得若干貫？
\switchcolumn
In the treasury there are three kinds of old currency: the first called
``full-bundle'' money (足陌錢), with 1000 wen per bundle; the second called
``nine-tenths'' money (九陌錢), with 900 wen per bundle; the third called
``eight-tenths'' money (八陌錢), with 800 wen per bundle. There are 500 guan
of full-bundle money, 300 guan of nine-tenths money, and 200 guan of
eight-tenths money. They are to be unified to a single standard. Question:
converting to the 1000-wen standard, how many guan are obtained in total?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
共得若干貫（足陌）。
\switchcolumn
The total obtained is a certain number of guan (full-bundle equivalent).
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以各陌錢數乘本陌法，得實際文數；并之；以足陌法一千除之，得共數。
\switchcolumn
Multiply each type of bundle money by its bundle rate to get the actual
number of wen; add them together; divide by the full-bundle standard of 1000
to get the total.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
足陌實數：$500 \times 1000 = 500{,}000$（文）

九陌實數：$300 \times 900 = 270{,}000$（文）

八陌實數：$200 \times 800 = 160{,}000$（文）

總實數：$500000 + 270000 + 160000 = 930{,}000$（文）

合足陌：$\dfrac{930000}{1000} = 930$（貫）
\switchcolumn
Full-bundle actual: $500 \times 1000 = 500{,}000$ (wen)

Nine-tenths actual: $300 \times 900 = 270{,}000$ (wen)

Eight-tenths actual: $200 \times 800 = 160{,}000$ (wen)

Total actual: $500{,}000 + 270{,}000 + 160{,}000 = 930{,}000$ (wen)

Converted to full-bundle: $\dfrac{930{,}000}{1000} = 930$ (guan)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 6: Setting Prices
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計定物價}{Setting Prices}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問有絲、綿、麻三物，共值八百貫。絲一斤價四貫，綿一斤價二貫，
麻一斤價五百文。欲買絲、綿、麻各若干斤，使價相等。問：各得幾何？
\switchcolumn
There are three commodities --- silk (絲), cotton floss (綿), and hemp (麻)
--- worth 800 guan in total. Silk costs 4 guan per jin, cotton floss 2 guan
per jin, and hemp 500 wen per jin. One wishes to buy certain quantities of
each so that the value of each commodity is equal. Question: how many jin of
each?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
絲若干斤，綿若干斤，麻若干斤。
\switchcolumn
A certain number of jin of silk, a certain number of jin of cotton floss,
and a certain number of jin of hemp.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以總價三而一，得每物之值。各以本價除之，得斤數。
\switchcolumn
Divide the total price by three to get the value of each commodity. Divide
by the respective price per jin to get the quantity in jin.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
每物之值：$\dfrac{800}{3} \approx 266.67$（貫）

絲斤數：$\dfrac{266.67}{4} \approx 66.67$（斤）

綿斤數：$\dfrac{266.67}{2} \approx 133.33$（斤）

麻斤數：$\dfrac{266.67}{0.5} = 533.33$（斤）
\switchcolumn
Value per commodity: $\dfrac{800}{3} \approx 266.67$ (guan)

Silk quantity: $\dfrac{266.67}{4} \approx 66.67$ (jin)

Cotton floss quantity: $\dfrac{266.67}{2} \approx 133.33$ (jin)

Hemp quantity: $\dfrac{266.67}{0.5} = 533.33$ (jin)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
%% PROBLEM 7: Profit on Transported Goods
%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\bilingualsubsection{計販運得利}{Profit on Transported Goods}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{問} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Question}}
\vspace{0.3em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
問一人持絹五百匹至遠方販賣。去時每匹價四貫，到彼時價漲五成。
回程買茶，茶價每斤三百文。問：賣絹得錢若干？可買茶若干斤？
\switchcolumn
A merchant carries 500 bolts of silk to a distant place for trade. The
purchase price was 4 guan per bolt; upon arrival, the price has risen by
50\%. On the return journey, he buys tea at 300 wen per jin. Question: how
much money does he obtain from selling the silk? How many jin of tea can he
buy?
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{答曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Answer}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
得錢若干貫，可買茶若干斤。
\switchcolumn
The money obtained is a certain number of guan; the tea that can be
purchased is a certain number of jin.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{術曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Method}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
以本價加利，得賣價；以匹數乘之，得總錢。以總錢除以茶價，得茶斤數。
\switchcolumn
Add the profit to the cost price to get the selling price; multiply by the
number of bolts to get the total money. Divide the total money by the tea
price to get the quantity of tea in jin.
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.4em}
\noindent\textbf{\textcolor{chinesecolor}{草曰} / \textcolor{englishcolor}{Working}}
\vspace{0.2em}
\begin{paracol}{2}
賣價：$4 \times 1.5 = 6$（貫/匹）

總錢：$500 \times 6 = 3000$（貫）$= 3{,}000{,}000$（文）

茶斤數：$\dfrac{3000000}{300} = 10000$（斤）
\switchcolumn
Selling price: $4 \times 1.5 = 6$ (guan/bolt)

Total money: $500 \times 6 = 3000$ (guan) $= 3{,}000{,}000$ (wen)

Tea quantity: $\dfrac{3{,}000{,}000}{300} = 10{,}000$ (jin)
\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}
\longline

%% ========================================================================
%% explanatory notes
%% ========================================================================
\newpage
\section*{Notes / 編者注}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Notes / 編者注}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\subsection*{Units of Measurement / 度量衡表}

The problems in these fascicles employ traditional Chinese units:
本編算題所用度量衡，皆依宋制：

\vspace{0.5em}

\begin{center}
\begin{longtable}{llll}
\toprule
\textbf{Unit} & \textbf{Chinese} & \textbf{Pinyin} & \textbf{Approx.~Modern Value} \\
\midrule
\endfirsthead
Length & 丈 & zhang & $\approx 3.33$ metres \\
Length & 尺 & chi & $\approx 33.3$ centimetres \\
Length & 寸 & cun & $\approx 3.33$ centimetres \\
Area & 平方丈 & pingfang zhang & $\approx 11.09$ square metres \\
Volume & 立方丈 & lifang zhang & $\approx 37.0$ cubic metres \\
Volume & 立方尺 & lifang chi & $\approx 37.0$ litres \\
Capacity & 石 & dan/shi & $\approx 60$ kilograms (grain) \\
Capacity & 升 & sheng & $\approx 0.6$ litres \\
Weight & 斤 & jin & $\approx 600$ grams (Song) \\
Weight & 兩 & liang & $\approx 37.5$ grams \\
Weight & 錢 & qian & $\approx 3.75$ grams \\
Currency & 貫 & guan & 1000 wen (cash coins) \\
Currency & 文 & wen & Single cash coin \\
Distance & 里 & li & $\approx 556$ metres \\
Distance & 步 & bu & $\approx 1.54$ metres \\
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}
\end{center}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\subsection*{Mathematical Methods / 數學方法}

\begin{paracol}{2}

數學九章所用算法，有數項獨創之術，尤以大衍求一術為最著。
本編營建、軍旅、市易三類，則多用體積公式、衰分術、重差術等。

\switchcolumn

The \textit{Shuxue Jiuzhang} employs several characteristic
mathematical techniques. The Construction, Military, and Trade fascicles
make extensive use of volume formulae, proportional distribution
(衰分), and the double-difference method (重差術).

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}

\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Frustum volume formula (塹堵/臺積術):} For a rectangular frustum
  with upper dimensions $a \times b$, lower dimensions $c \times d$, and height $h$:
  \[
  V = \frac{h}{3}(ab + cd + ad + bc)
  \]
  This is equivalent to the modern prismatoid formula.

  \item \textbf{Proportional distribution (衰分/均輸術):} Problems involving
  allocation according to given ratios. Each party contributes in proportion
to their respective holding or capacity.

  \item \textbf{Double-difference method (重差術):} A trigonometric surveying
technique using two observations to determine distances and heights.

  \item \textbf{Currency conversion (陌法):} The ``bundle'' (陌) system of
currency conversion, reflecting the debased coinage of the Southern Song
period. One ``bundle'' (陌) nominally contained 1000 wen, but in practice
bundles of 900 or 800 wen were common.
\end{enumerate}

%% ------------------------------------------------------------------------
\subsection*{Historical Context / 歷史背景}

\begin{paracol}{2}

秦九韶，字道古，生於南宋寧宗嘉泰二年（1202），卒於理宗景定二年（1261）。
所撰《數學九章》成書於淳祐七年（1247），時宋室南渡已百餘年，
國勢日蹙，蒙古鐵騎壓境。卷八軍旅之題，正反映當時岌岌可危之軍事情勢。

\switchcolumn

Qin Jiushao (ca.~1202--1261), styled Daogu, was one of the foremost
mathematicians of the Song Dynasty. The \textit{Shuxue Jiuzhang} was
completed in 1247, during the turbulent final decades of the Southern Song.
The military problems in Fascicle 8 directly reflect the desperate military
situation of the time, as the Song faced existential threats from the Mongols
under Genghis Khan and later Kublai Khan.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}

\begin{paracol}{2}

卷九市易之題，則反映南宋繁榮之商業經濟：紙幣交子會子之流通、
官營鹽茶之專賣、長途販運之興盛。此皆中古時期世界最發達之商業體系。

\switchcolumn

The trade problems in Fascicle 9 reflect the sophisticated commercial economy
of the Song, with its paper currency (交子, 會子), government monopolies on
salt and tea, and extensive long-distance trade networks. This was one of the
most advanced commercial systems in the medieval world.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{0.5em}

\begin{paracol}{2}

秦九韶自述其書：「大則可以通神明，順性命；小則可以經世務，類萬物。」
數學九章不獨為數學之鉅著，亦為研究宋代社會經濟軍事之重要史料。

\switchcolumn

Qin Jiushao described his own work: ``In the large sense it can communicate
with the divine and accord with the nature of things; in the small sense it
can govern worldly affairs and classify all things.'' The \textit{Shuxue
Jiuzhang} is not only a masterpiece of mathematics, but also an
important historical source for studying the society, economy, and military
of the Song Dynasty.

\end{paracol}

\vspace{2em}
\begin{center}
\longline
\vspace{1em}
{\small\color{sectioncolor}
\textit{--- End of Fascicles 7--9 / 卷七至卷九終 ---}
}
\end{center}
